        Project directory:

        Idea:
Build a fail-closed generalized payment subsystem for ArcCompute-style payouts on Solana. Replace direct grant sends with a reusable payment ledger, provider abstraction, wallet registry, Discord confirmation flow, manual hold/retry controls, and restart-safe recovery. Critical requirements: no automatic double-payment after tx submission unless chain definitively reports failure; ambiguous chain state freezes to manual hold; private keys never enter DB/logs/admin-chat; atomic worker claiming; separate test-payment and final-payment attempts; grants becomes a producer into the payment system.

User notes and answers:
- Locked decisions from user: test payment is a fixed tiny global amount; confirmation destination is route-configured and may be the source thread/forum post or a specific payments channel/forum; manual admin controls for retry/hold/release/cancel/status are required; do not auto-fallback from Codex to Claude on backend connection failures.

  "watch_items": [
    "Fail-closed invariant is the top priority: any ambiguous post-submit state or confirmation timeout must land in `manual_hold`, never in auto-retry.",
    "Persist `tx_signature` and send-phase audit data before waiting on confirmation; do not leave a submitted payment recoverable only from memory.",
    "Keep `payment_requests`, `wallet_registry`, and `payment_channel_routes` off generic admin-table querying; dedicated tools must redact wallet addresses and never expose key material.",
    "Do not log, serialize, or store private keys anywhere; the provider must only hold the in-memory `SolanaClient`/keypair already used by the grants flow.",
    "Route resolution must mirror existing social-route behavior exactly: exact channel, then parent channel, then guild default; only if nothing resolves should grants fall back to the source thread.",
    "Test and final payments must remain distinct ledger entries linked by `(producer, producer_ref, is_test)` so restart and recovery logic can disambiguate phases.",
    "`release_payment` must remain the only path out of `manual_hold`; `retry_payment` must remain blocked for `submitted` and `manual_hold`.",
    "Update both the runtime logic and the admin-chat discoverability layer; adding tools without updating `agent.py` leaves the controls effectively unreachable.",
    "`payment_requested` must be treated as an active grant state everywhere that gates duplicate applications, not just in the grants cog happy path.",
    "Persistent Discord confirmations are a known verification gap in the approved plan; add lightweight automation for `PaymentConfirmView`/`bot.add_view(...)` recovery if feasible and do not regress it during implementation.",
    "Mirror the existing `social_publications` queue and `social_channel_routes` patterns rather than inventing a second queue/routing style; the repo already has debt from mixed DB access patterns.",
    "Supabase RPC behavior is a known repo-wide risk area; validate the new claim RPC carefully and keep the implementation close to the working social-publications template.",
    "Do not introduce any Codex-to-Claude automatic fallback behavior on backend failures; the user explicitly locked that out.",
    "Preserve historical grant payout columns for old records, but ensure new payment execution flows only through the payment ledger."
  ],

text,\n+    created_at timestamptz not null default timezone('utc', now()),\n+    updated_at timestamptz not null default timezone('utc', now()),\n+    check (char_length(btrim(producer)) > 0),\n+    check (char_length(btrim(producer_ref)) > 0),\n+    check (char_length(btrim(recipient_wallet)) > 0),\n+    check (char_length(btrim(chain)) > 0),\n+    check (char_length(btrim(provider)) > 0),\n+    check (confirmed_by is null or char_length(btrim(confirmed_by)) > 0),\n+    check (not is_test or (amount_usd is null and token_price_usd is null))\n+);\n+\n+create unique index if not exists uq_payment_requests_active_producer_ref\n+    on public.payment_requests (producer, producer_ref, is_test)\n+    where status not in ('failed', 'cancelled');\n+\n+create unique index if not exists uq_payment_requests_tx_signature\n+    on public.payment_requests (tx_signature)\n+    where tx_signature is not null;\n+\n+create index if not exists idx_payment_requests_guild_status\n+    on public.payment_requests (guild_id, status, scheduled_at);\n+\n+create index if not exists idx_payment_requests_due_queue\n+    on public.payment_requests (scheduled_at, created_at)\n+    where status = 'queued';\n+\n+create index if not exists idx_payment_requests_producer_ref\n+    on public.payment_requests (producer, producer_ref, is_test);\n+\n+create index if not exists idx_payment_requests_wallet_status\n+    on public.payment_requests (wallet_id, status)\n+    where wallet_id is not null;\n+\n+create index if not exists idx_payment_requests_pending_confirmation\n+    on public.payment_requests (guild_id, confirm_channel_id, confirm_thread_id, created_at)\n+    where status = 'pending_confirmation';\n+\n+drop trigger if exists payment_requests_set_updated_at on public.payment_requests;\n+create trigger payment_requests_set_updated_at\n+before update on public.payment_requests\n+for each row\n+execute function public.set_payment_updated_at();\n+\n+create or replace function public.claim_due_payment_requests(\n+    claim_limit integer default 10,\n+    claim_guild_ids bigint[] default null\n+)\n+returns setof public.payment_requests\n+language plpgsql\n+as $$\n+begin\n+    return query\n+    with due_rows as (\n+        select pr.payment_id\n+        from public.payment_requests pr\n+        where pr.status = 'queued'\n+          and pr.scheduled_at <= timezone('utc', now())\n+          and (pr.retry_after is null or pr.retry_after <= timezone('utc', now()))\n+          and (claim_guild_ids is null or pr.guild_id = any(claim_guild_ids))\n+        order by coalesce(pr.retry_after, pr.scheduled_at), pr.created_at\n+        for update skip locked\n+        limit greatest(claim_limit, 0)\n+    ),\n+    updated_rows as (\n+        update public.payment_requests pr\n+        set status = 'processing',\n+            attempt_count = coalesce(pr.attempt_count, 0) + 1,\n+            retry_after = null,\n+            updated_at = timezone('utc', now())\n+        from due_rows\n+        where pr.payment_id = due_rows.payment_id\n+        returning pr.*\n+    )\n+    select * from updated_rows;\n+end;\n+$$;\n+\n+alter table public.wallet_registry enable row level security;\n+alter table public.payment_channel_routes enable row level security;\n+alter table public.payment_requests enable row level security;\n+\n+revoke all on public.wallet_registry from anon, authenticated;\n+revoke all on public.payment_channel_routes from anon, authenticated;\n+revoke all on public.payment_requests from anon, authenticated;\n+revoke execute on function public.claim_due_payment_requests(integer, bigint[]) from anon, authenticated;\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc

        Idea:
Build a fail-closed generalized payment subsystem for ArcCompute-style payouts on Solana. Replace direct grant sends with a reusable payment ledger, provider abstraction, wallet registry, Discord confirmation flow, manual hold/retry controls, and restart-safe recovery. Critical requirements: no automatic double-payment after tx submission unless chain definitively reports failure; ambiguous chain state freezes to manual hold; private keys never enter DB/logs/admin-chat; atomic worker claiming; separate test-payment and final-payment attempts; grants becomes a producer into the payment system.

User notes and answers:
- Locked decisions from user: test payment is a fixed tiny global amount; confirmation destination is route-configured and may be the source thread/forum post or a specific payments channel/forum; manual admin controls for retry/hold/release/cancel/status are required; do not auto-fallback from Codex to Claude on backend connection failures.

  "watch_items": [
    "Fail-closed invariant is the top priority: any ambiguous post-submit state or confirmation timeout must land in `manual_hold`, never in auto-retry.",
    "Persist `tx_signature` and send-phase audit data before waiting on confirmation; do not leave a submitted payment recoverable only from memory.",
    "Keep `payment_requests`, `wallet_registry`, and `payment_channel_routes` off generic admin-table querying; dedicated tools must redact wallet addresses and never expose key material.",
    "Do not log, serialize, or store private keys anywhere; the provider must only hold the in-memory `SolanaClient`/keypair already used by the grants flow.",
    "Route resolution must mirror existing social-route behavior exactly: exact channel, then parent channel, then guild default; only if nothing resolves should grants fall back to the source thread.",
    "Test and final payments must remain distinct ledger entries linked by `(producer, producer_ref, is_test)` so restart and recovery logic can disambiguate phases.",
    "`release_payment` must remain the only path out of `manual_hold`; `retry_payment` must remain blocked for `submitted` and `manual_hold`.",
    "Update both the runtime logic and the admin-chat discoverability layer; adding tools without updating `agent.py` leaves the controls effectively unreachable.",
    "`payment_requested` must be treated as an active grant state everywhere that gates duplicate applications, not just in the grants cog happy path.",
    "Persistent Discord confirmations are a known verification gap in the approved plan; add lightweight automation for `PaymentConfirmView`/`bot.add_view(...)` recovery if feasible and do not regress it during implementation.",
    "Mirror the existing `social_publications` queue and `social_channel_routes` patterns rather than inventing a second queue/routing style; the repo already has debt from mixed DB access patterns.",
    "Supabase RPC behavior is a known repo-wide risk area; validate the new claim RPC carefully and keep the implementation close to the working social-publications template.",
    "Do not introduce any Codex-to-Claude automatic fallback behavior on backend failures; the user explicitly locked that out.",
    "Preserve historical grant payout columns for old records, but ensure new payment execution flows only through the payment ledger."
  ],

> AGENT

The handler work is moving from the social-publications pattern into a payment-specific section now. I’m adding guild-scoped wallet, route, ledger, transition, and recovery helpers with explicit state guards so later admin controls and the worker can share one safe path.

> AGENT

The provider layer is next. I’m keeping it deliberately thin: one dataclass contract, one abstract interface, and a Solana adapter that treats any exception after the actual send call as ambiguous so the service can fail closed.

             'status': 'queued',\n+                'tx_signature': None,\n+                'send_phase': None,\n+                'submitted_at': None,\n+                'completed_at': None,\n+                'retry_after': retry_after,\n+            },\n+            guild_id=guild_id,\n+            allowed_statuses=['failed'],\n+        )\n+\n+    def release_payment_hold(\n+        self,\n+        payment_id: str,\n+        new_status: str,\n+        guild_id: Optional[int] = None,\n+        reason: Optional[str] = None,\n+    ) -> bool:\n+        \"\"\"Release a manual_hold payment only to confirmed, failed, or keep holding.\"\"\"\n+        if new_status not in {'confirmed', 'failed', 'manual_hold'}:\n+            logger.warning(f\"Unsupported release_payment_hold target status: {new_status}\")\n+            return False\n+\n+        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {'status': new_status}\n+        if new_status in {'confirmed', 'failed'}:\n+            payload['completed_at'] = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n+            payload['retry_after'] = None\n+        else:\n+            payload['completed_at'] = None\n+        if reason is not None:\n+            payload['last_error'] = reason\n+        elif new_status == 'confirmed':\n+            payload['last_error'] = None\n+\n+        return self._update_payment_request_record(\n+            payment_id,\n+            payload,\n+            guild_id=guild_id,\n+            allowed_statuses=['manual_hold'],\n+        )\n+\n+    def cancel_payment(\n+        self,\n+        payment_id: str,\n+        guild_id: Optional[int] = None,\n+        reason: Optional[str] = None,\n+    ) -> bool:\n+        \"\"\"Cancel a payment only from non-submitted safe states.\"\"\"\n+        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {\n+            'status': 'cancelled',\n+            'completed_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc),\n+            'retry_after': None,\n+        }\n+        if reason is not None:\n+            payload['last_error'] = reason\n+        return self._update_payment_request_record(\n+            payment_id,\n+            payload,\n+            guild_id=guild_id,\n+            allowed_statuses=['pending_confirmation', 'queued', 'failed', 'manual_hold'],\n+        )\n+\n+    def claim_due_payment_requests(\n+        self,\n+        limit: int = 10,\n+        guild_ids: Optional[List[int]] = None,\n+    ) -> List[Dict]:\n+        \"\"\"Atomically claim due queued payment requests through the Supabase RPC.\"\"\"\n+        if not self.supabase:\n+            return []\n+\n+        writable_guild_ids = self._get_writable_guild_ids(guild_ids)\n+        if writable_guild_ids == []:\n+            return []\n+\n+        try:\n+            params: Dict[str, Any] = {'claim_limit': limit}\n+            if writable_guild_ids is not None:\n+                params['claim_guild_ids'] = writable_guild_ids\n+            result = self.supabase.rpc('claim_due_payment_requests', params).execute()\n+            return result.data or []\n+        except Exception as e:\n+            logger.error(f\"Error claiming due payment requests: {e}\", exc_info=True)\n+            return []\n+\n+    def get_inflight_payment_requests_for_recovery(\n+        self,\n+        guild_ids: Optional[List[int]] = None,\n+    ) -> List[Dict]:\n+        \"\"\"Fetch processing/submitted payments that need restart-time recovery.\"\"\"\n+        writable_guild_ids = self._get_writable_guild_ids(guild_ids)\n+        if writable_guild_ids == [] or not self.supabase:\n+            return []\n+\n+        try:\n+            query = self.supabase.table('payment_requests').select('*')\n+            if writable_guild_ids and len(writable_guild_ids) == 1:\n+                query = query.eq('guild_id', writable_guild_ids[0])\n+            result = query.order('updated_at').execute()\n+            rows = result.data or []\n+            allowed_statuses = {'processing', 'submitted'}\n+            return [\n+                row for row in rows\n+                if row.get('status') in allowed_statuses\n+                and (writable_guild_ids is None or row.get('guild_id') in writable_guild_ids)\n+            ]\n+        except Exception as e:\n+            logger.error(\"Error fetching inflight payment requests for recovery: %s\", e, exc_info=True)\n+            return []\n+\n+    def get_pending_confirmation_payments(\n+        self,\n+        guild_ids: Optional[List[int]] = None,\n+    ) -> List[Dict]:\n+        \"\"\"Fetch pending confirmations for persistent Discord view re-registration.\"\"\"\n+        writable_guild_ids = self._get_writable_guild_ids(guild_ids)\n+        if writable_guild_ids == [] or not self.supabase:\n+            return []\n+\n+        try:\n+            query = self.supabase.table('payment_requests').select('*')\n+            if writable_guild_ids and len(writable_guild_ids) == 1:\n+                query = query.eq('guild_id', writable_guild_ids[0])\n+            result = query.order('created_at').execute()\n+            rows = result.data or []\n+            return [\n+                row for row in rows\n+                if row.get('status') == 'pending_confirmation'\n+                and (writable_guild_ids is None or row.get('guild_id') in writable_guild_ids)\n+            ]\n+        except Exception as e:\n+            logger.error(\"Error fetching pending confirmation payments: %s\", e, exc_info=True)\n+            return []\n+\n     def mark_member_first_shared(self, member_id: int, guild_id: Optional[int] = None) -> bool:\n         \"\"\"Set first_shared_at timestamp for a member (only if not already set).\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
Build a fail-closed generalized payment subsystem for ArcCompute-style payouts on Solana. Replace direct grant sends with a reusable payment ledger, provider abstraction, wallet registry, Discord confirmation flow, manual hold/retry controls, and restart-safe recovery. Critical requirements: no automatic double-payment after tx submission unless chain definitively reports failure; ambiguous chain state freezes to manual hold; private keys never enter DB/logs/admin-chat; atomic worker claiming; separate test-payment and final-payment attempts; grants becomes a producer into the payment system.

User notes and answers:
- Locked decisions from user: test payment is a fixed tiny global amount; confirmation destination is route-configured and may be the source thread/forum post or a specific payments channel/forum; manual admin controls for retry/hold/release/cancel/status are required; do not auto-fallback from Codex to Claude on backend connection failures.

    "Fail-closed invariant is the top priority: any ambiguous post-submit state or confirmation timeout must land in `manual_hold`, never in auto-retry.",
    "Persist `tx_signature` and send-phase audit data before waiting on confirmation; do not leave a submitted payment recoverable only from memory.",
    "Keep `payment_requests`, `wallet_registry`, and `payment_channel_routes` off generic admin-table querying; dedicated tools must redact wallet addresses and never expose key material.",
    "Do not log, serialize, or store private keys anywhere; the provider must only hold the in-memory `SolanaClient`/keypair already used by the grants flow.",
    "Route resolution must mirror existing social-route behavior exactly: exact channel, then parent channel, then guild default; only if nothing resolves should grants fall back to the source thread.",
    "Test and final payments must remain distinct ledger entries linked by `(producer, producer_ref, is_test)` so restart and recovery logic can disambiguate phases.",
    "`release_payment` must remain the only path out of `manual_hold`; `retry_payment` must remain blocked for `submitted` and `manual_hold`.",
    "Update both the runtime logic and the admin-chat discoverability layer; adding tools without updating `agent.py` leaves the controls effectively unreachable.",

        Idea:
Build a fail-closed generalized payment subsystem for ArcCompute-style payouts on Solana. Replace direct grant sends with a reusable payment ledger, provider abstraction, wallet registry, Discord confirmation flow, manual hold/retry controls, and restart-safe recovery. Critical requirements: no automatic double-payment after tx submission unless chain definitively reports failure; ambiguous chain state freezes to manual hold; private keys never enter DB/logs/admin-chat; atomic worker claiming; separate test-payment and final-payment attempts; grants becomes a producer into the payment system.

User notes and answers:
- Locked decisions from user: test payment is a fixed tiny global amount; confirmation destination is route-configured and may be the source thread/forum post or a specific payments channel/forum; manual admin controls for retry/hold/release/cancel/status are required; do not auto-fallback from Codex to Claude on backend connection failures.

  "watch_items": [
    "Fail-closed invariant is the top priority: any ambiguous post-submit state or confirmation timeout must land in `manual_hold`, never in auto-retry.",
    "Persist `tx_signature` and send-phase audit data before waiting on confirmation; do not leave a submitted payment recoverable only from memory.",
    "Keep `payment_requests`, `wallet_registry`, and `payment_channel_routes` off generic admin-table querying; dedicated tools must redact wallet addresses and never expose key material.",
    "Do not log, serialize, or store private keys anywhere; the provider must only hold the in-memory `SolanaClient`/keypair already used by the grants flow.",
    "Route resolution must mirror existing social-route behavior exactly: exact channel, then parent channel, then guild default; only if nothing resolves should grants fall back to the source thread.",
    "Test and final payments must remain distinct ledger entries linked by `(producer, producer_ref, is_test)` so restart and recovery logic can disambiguate phases.",
    "`release_payment` must remain the only path out of `manual_hold`; `retry_payment` must remain blocked for `submitted` and `manual_hold`.",
    "Update both the runtime logic and the admin-chat discoverability layer; adding tools without updating `agent.py` leaves the controls effectively unreachable.",
    "`payment_requested` must be treated as an active grant state everywhere that gates duplicate applications, not just in the grants cog happy path.",
    "Persistent Discord confirmations are a known verification gap in the approved plan; add lightweight automation for `PaymentConfirmView`/`bot.add_view(...)` recovery if feasible and do not regress it during implementation.",
    "Mirror the existing `social_publications` queue and `social_channel_routes` patterns rather than inventing a second queue/routing style; the repo already has debt from mixed DB access patterns.",
    "Supabase RPC behavior is a known repo-wide risk area; validate the new claim RPC carefully and keep the implementation close to the working social-publications template.",
    "Do not introduce any Codex-to-Claude automatic fallback behavior on backend failures; the user explicitly locked that out.",
    "Preserve historical grant payout columns for old records, but ensure new payment execution flows only through the payment ledger."
  ],

= await service.execute_payment(\"pay-1\")\n+\n+    assert result[\"status\"] == \"confirmed\"\n+    assert db_handler.rows[\"pay-1\"][\"tx_signature\"] == \"sig-123\"\n+    assert ambiguous_result[\"last_error\"]\n+\n+    timeout_db = FakePaymentDB()\n+    timeout_db.rows[\"pay-timeout\"] = {\n+        \"payment_id\": \"pay-timeout\",\n+        \"guild_id\": 3,\n+         Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc

        Idea:
Build a fail-closed generalized payment subsystem for ArcCompute-style payouts on Solana. Replace direct grant sends with a reusable payment ledger, provider abstraction, wallet registry, Discord confirmation flow, manual hold/retry controls, and restart-safe recovery. Critical requirements: no automatic double-payment after tx submission unless chain definitively reports failure; ambiguous chain state freezes to manual hold; private keys never enter DB/logs/admin-chat; atomic worker claiming; separate test-payment and final-payment attempts; grants becomes a producer into the payment system.

User notes and answers:
- Locked decisions from user: test payment is a fixed tiny global amount; confirmation destination is route-configured and may be the source thread/forum post or a specific payments channel/forum; manual admin controls for retry/hold/release/cancel/status are required; do not auto-fallback from Codex to Claude on backend connection failures.

  "watch_items": [
    "Fail-closed invariant is the top priority: any ambiguous post-submit state or confirmation timeout must land in `manual_hold`, never in auto-retry.",
    "Persist `tx_signature` and send-phase audit data before waiting on confirmation; do not leave a submitted payment recoverable only from memory.",
    "Keep `payment_requests`, `wallet_registry`, and `payment_channel_routes` off generic admin-table querying; dedicated tools must redact wallet addresses and never expose key material.",
    "Do not log, serialize, or store private keys anywhere; the provider must only hold the in-memory `SolanaClient`/keypair already used by the grants flow.",
    "Route resolution must mirror existing social-route behavior exactly: exact channel, then parent channel, then guild default; only if nothing resolves should grants fall back to the source thread.",
    "Test and final payments must remain distinct ledger entries linked by `(producer, producer_ref, is_test)` so restart and recovery logic can disambiguate phases.",
    "`release_payment` must remain the only path out of `manual_hold`; `retry_payment` must remain blocked for `submitted` and `manual_hold`.",
    "Update both the runtime logic and the admin-chat discoverability layer; adding tools without updating `agent.py` leaves the controls effectively unreachable.",
    "`payment_requested` must be treated as an active grant state everywhere that gates duplicate applications, not just in the grants cog happy path.",
    "Persistent Discord confirmations are a known verification gap in the approved plan; add lightweight automation for `PaymentConfirmView`/`bot.add_view(...)` recovery if feasible and do not regress it during implementation.",
    "Mirror the existing `social_publications` queue and `social_channel_routes` patterns rather than inventing a second queue/routing style; the repo already has debt from mixed DB access patterns.",
    "Supabase RPC behavior is a known repo-wide risk area; validate the new claim RPC carefully and keep the implementation close to the working social-publications template.",
    "Do not introduce any Codex-to-Claude automatic fallback behavior on backend failures; the user explicitly locked that out.",
    "Preserve historical grant payout columns for old records, but ensure new payment execution flows only through the payment ledger."

  "meta_commentary": "Execute in the listed order and do not blur the safety boundaries to save time. The highest-risk mistakes are all state-machine mistakes: if a payment may have been submitted, freeze it; if the chain definitively rejected it, then and only then make it retryable. Mirror the existing social queue/routing patterns closely so the new subsystem fits repo conventions and does not deepen the current DB-access debt. Treat admin-chat exposure as part of the core implementation, not a follow-up, because the current generic table query surface is unsafe for new wallet/payment tables. For testing, the remaining critique from planning was weak automation around persistent-view recovery, so add a lightweight assertion for `PaymentConfirmView` re-registration if the existing harness makes that practical. The approved plan referenced a new dedicated payment test file, but this finalize brief intentionally adapts that into extending the existing test suite because the execution constraint here is to avoid creating new test files while still delivering equivalent coverage and the same safety checks.",

self._handle_terminal_payment(payment)\n+\n+    async def send_confirmation_request(self, payment_id: str) -> Optional[discord.Message]:\n+        \"\"\"Post one confirmation message with a persistent view.\"\"\"\n+        payment = self.db_handler.get_payment_request(payment_id)\n+        if not payment:\n+            return None\n+        if payment.get('status') != 'pending_confirmation':\n+            return None\n+\n+        destination = await self._resolve_destination(\n+            payment.get('confirm_channel_id'),\n+            payment.get('confirm_thread_id'),\n+        )\n+        if destination is None:\n+            logger.warning(\n+                \"[PaymentCog] Could not resolve confirmation destination for payment %s\",\n+                payment_id,\n+            )\n+            return None\n+\n+        view = PaymentConfirmView(self, payment_id)\n+        self.bot.add_view(view)\n+        return await self._send_message(\n+            destination,\n+            self._build_confirmation_message(payment),\n+            view=view,\n+        )\n+\n+    async def _process_claimed_payment(self, payment: Dict[str, Any]):\n+        payment_id = payment.get('payment_id')\n+        guild_id = payment.get('guild_id')\n+        if not payment_id:\n+            return\n+\n+        try:\n+            result = await self.payment_service.execute_payment(payment_id, guild_id=guild_id)\n+        except Exception as exc:\n+            logger.error(\n+                \"[PaymentCog] Unexpected error while executing payment %s: %s\",\n+                payment_id,\n+                exc,\n+                exc_info=True,\n+            )\n+            current = self.db_handler.get_payment_request(payment_id, guild_id=guild_id)\n+            if current and current.get('status') in {'processing', 'submitted'}:\n+                self.db_handler.mark_payment_manual_hold(\n+                    payment_id,\n+                    reason='Worker hit an unexpected error; payment requires manual review',\n+                    guild_id=guild_id,\n+                )\n+                current = self.db_handler.get_payment_request(payment_id, guild_id=guild_id)\n+            result = current\n+\n+        if result and self._is_terminal(result):\n+            await self._handle_terminal_payment(result)\n+\n+    async def _handle_terminal_payment(self, payment: Dict[str, Any]):\n+        await self._notify_payment_result(payment)\n+        await self._handoff_terminal_result(payment)\n+\n+    async def _notify_payment_result(self, payment: Dict[str, Any]):\n+        destination = await self._resolve_destination(\n+            payment.get('notify_channel_id'),\n+            payment.get('notify_thread_id'),\n+        )\n+        if destination is None:\n+            logger.warning(\n+                \"[PaymentCog] Could not resolve notify destination for payment %s\",\n+                payment.get('payment_id'),\n+            )\n+            return\n+\n+        await self._send_message(destination, self._build_result_message(payment))\n+\n+    async def _handoff_terminal_result(self, payment: Dict[str, Any]):\n+        producer = str(payment.get('producer') or '').strip().lower()\n+        if not producer:\n+            return\n+\n+        cog = None\n+        for candidate in self._candidate_producer_cog_names(producer):\n+            cog = self.bot.get_cog(candidate)\n+            if cog:\n+                break\n+        if cog is None or not hasattr(cog, 'handle_payment_result'):\n+            return\n+\n+        try:\n+            result = cog.handle_payment_result(payment)\n+            if inspect.isawaitable(result):\n+                await result\n+        except Exception as exc:\n+            logger.error(\n+                \"[PaymentCog] Producer handoff failed for payment %s (%s): %s\",\n+                payment.get('payment_id'),\n+                producer,\n+                exc,\n+                exc_info=True,\n+            )\n+\n+    def _candidate_producer_cog_names(self, producer: str) -> List[str]:\n+        title = ''.join(part.capitalize() for part in producer.split('_'))\n+        return [\n+            f\"{title}Cog\",\n+            f\"{producer.capitalize()}Cog\",\n+            title,\n+            producer,\n+        ]\n+\n+    async def _resolve_destination(\n+        self,\n+        channel_id: Optional[int],\n+        thread_id: Optional[int],\n+    ) -> Optional[discord.abc.Messageable]:\n+        target_id = int(thread_id) if thread_id else int(channel_id) if channel_id else None\n+        if target_id is None:\n+            return None\n+\n+        destination = self.bot.get_channel(target_id)\n+        if destination is not None:\n+            return destination\n+\n+        try:\n+            return await self.bot.fetch_channel(target_id)\n+        except Exception as exc:\n+            logger.warning(\"[PaymentCog] Failed to fetch destination %s: %s\", target_id, exc)\n+            return None\n+\n+    async def _send_message(\n+        self,\n+        destination: discord.abc.Messageable,\n+        content: str,\n+        *,\n+        view: Optional[discord.ui.View] = None,\n+    ) -> Optional[discord.Message]:\n+        rate_limiter = getattr(self.bot, 'rate_limiter', None)\n+        if rate_limiter is not None:\n+            return await safe_send_message(\n+                self.bot,\n+                destination,\n+                rate_limiter,\n+                logger,\n+                content=content,\n+                view=view,\n+            )\n+        return await destination.send(content, view=view)\n+\n+    def _build_confirmation_message(self, payment: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n+        amount = float(payment.get('amount_token') or 0)\n+        payment_type = 'test payment' if payment.get('is_test') else 'final payment'\n+        return (\n+            f\"**Payment confirmation required**\\n\"\n+            f\"- Payment ID: `{payment.get('payment_id')}`\\n\"\n+            f\"- Producer: `{payment.get('producer')}` / `{payment.get('producer_ref')}`\\n\"\n+            f\"- Type: {payment_type}\\n\"\n+            f\"- Amount: {amount:.8f} {self._token_label(payment)}\\n\"\n+            f\"- Wallet: `{_redact_wallet(payment.get('recipient_wallet'))}`\\n\"\n+            f\"- Route: `{payment.get('route_key') or 'unrouted'}`\"\n+        )\n+\n+    def _build_result_message(self, payment: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n+        amount = float(payment.get('amount_token') or 0)\n+        status = str(payment.get('status') or 'unknown')\n+        lines = [\n+            f\"**Payment {status.replace('_', ' ').title()}**\",\n+            f\"- Payment ID: `{payment.get('payment_id')}`\",\n+            f\"- Producer: `{payment.get('producer')}` / `{payment.get('producer_ref')}`\",\n+            f\"- Type: {'test payment' if payment.get('is_test') else 'final payment'}\",\n+            f\"- Amount: {amount:.8f} {self._token_label(payment)}\",\n+            f\"- Wallet: `{_redact_wallet(payment.get('recipient_wallet'))}`\",\n+        ]\n+        if payment.get('tx_signature'):\n+            lines.append(f\"- Transaction: `{payment.get('tx_signature')}`\")\n+        if payment.get('last_error'):\n+            lines.append(f\"- Detail: {payment.get('last_error')}\")\n+        return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n+\n+    def _token_label(self, payment: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n+        provider = None\n+        provider_name = payment.get('provider')\n+        if provider_name and self.payment_service:\n+            provider = self.payment_service.providers.get(str(provider_name).strip().lower())\n+        if provider is not None:\n+            try:\n+                return provider.token_name()\n+            except Exception:\n+                pass\n+        chain = str(payment.get('chain') or '').strip().upper()\n+        return chain or 'TOKEN'\n+\n+    def _get_writable_guild_ids(self) -> Optional[List[int]]:\n+        server_config = getattr(self.db_handler, 'server_config', None)\n+        if not server_config:\n+            return None\n+        guild_ids = [int(server['guild_id']) for server in server_config.get_enabled_servers(require_write=True)]\n+        return guild_ids or None\n+\n+    def _is_terminal(self, payment: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> bool:\n+        if not payment:\n+            return False\n+        return payment.get('status') in {'confirmed', 'failed', 'manual_hold', 'cancelled'}\n+\n+\n+async def setup(bot: commands.Bot):\n+    db_handler = getattr(bot, 'db_handler', None)\n+    payment_service = getattr(bot, 'payment_service', None)\n+    if db_handler is None or payment_service is None:\n+        logger.error(\"PaymentCog setup skipped because db_handler or payment_service is missing.\")\n+        return\n+    await bot.add_cog(PaymentCog(bot, db_handler, payment_service=payment_service))\n*** End Patch\n"
}

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc/src/features/admin_chat/agent.py\n@@\n - share_to_social(message_id or message_link, tweet_text?, action?, target_post?, reply_to_tweet?, text_only?, schedule_for?, platform?) — share or schedule publishing to X/Twitter. Supports post, reply, and retweet actions. `schedule_for` must be ISO-8601 with timezone. `target_post` or `reply_to_tweet` accepts a tweet URL or ID. **Thread replies default to text-only** so a follow-up doesn't reattach the parent tweet's media — pass `text_only=false` explicitly if you DO want a reply to also include the source message's media. Immediate success returns `tweet_url`/`tweet_id` plus `publication_id`; scheduled success returns `publication_id` with queued status. If `already_shared` is true on a rerun, cite the returned existing `tweet_url` instead of treating it as a failure.\n - list_social_routes(platform?, channel_id?, enabled?, limit?) — inspect configured social routes for the active guild.\n - create_social_route(platform?, channel_id?, enabled?, route_config?) — create a guild-default or channel-specific route. Omit channel_id for the guild default. Put outbound details in `route_config`, for example `{\"account\": \"main\"}`.\n - update_social_route(route_id, platform?, channel_id?, enabled?, route_config?) — update one route by id. Pass `channel_id=\"\"` to convert it to the guild-default route.\n - delete_social_route(route_id) — delete one route by id.\n+- list_payment_routes(producer?, channel_id?, enabled?, limit?) — inspect configured payment routes for the active guild.\n+- create_payment_route(producer, channel_id?, enabled?, route_config?) — create a guild-default or channel-specific payment route. Put confirm/notify routing details in `route_config`, for example `{\"use_source_thread\": true}` or explicit channel/thread IDs.\n+- update_payment_route(route_id, producer?, channel_id?, enabled?, route_config?) — update one payment route by id. Pass `channel_id=\"\"` to convert it to the guild-default route.\n+- delete_payment_route(route_id) — delete one payment route by id.\n+- list_wallets(chain?, discord_user_id?, verified?, limit?) — inspect registered payout wallets with redacted addresses.\n+- list_payments(status?, producer?, recipient_discord_id?, wallet_id?, is_test?, route_key?, limit?) — inspect payment ledger rows with redacted wallet addresses.\n+- get_payment_status(payment_id) — inspect one payment row with redacted wallet address, status, transaction signature, and errors.\n+- retry_payment(payment_id) — move a failed payment back to queued. This is blocked for submitted/manual_hold rows.\n+- hold_payment(payment_id, reason) — force a payment into manual_hold.\n+- release_payment(payment_id, new_status, reason?) — release a manual_hold payment to confirmed, failed, or keep it held.\n+- cancel_payment(payment_id, reason?) — cancel a payment that has not entered submitted/manual_hold.\n - update_member_socials(user_id, twitter_url?, reddit_url?) — set/clear a member's Twitter or Reddit handle on file. The Twitter handle is what social picks use to auto-tag the creator. Pass an empty string to clear a field; omit a field to leave it alone. Use this whenever the admin says things like \"set X's twitter to @y\" or \"X is @y on twitter\".\n - resolve_user(username) — get a user's Discord ID and mention tag.\n@@\n - Manage channel-to-social routing with `list_social_routes`, `create_social_route`, `update_social_route`, and `delete_social_route` instead of editing `social_channel_routes` manually when possible.\n+- Manage payment routing and payment state with the dedicated payment tools instead of querying payment tables directly. Those tools intentionally redact wallet addresses.\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
Build a fail-closed generalized payment subsystem for ArcCompute-style payouts on Solana. Replace direct grant sends with a reusable payment ledger, provider abstraction, wallet registry, Discord confirmation flow, manual hold/retry controls, and restart-safe recovery. Critical requirements: no automatic double-payment after tx submission unless chain definitively reports failure; ambiguous chain state freezes to manual hold; private keys never enter DB/logs/admin-chat; atomic worker claiming; separate test-payment and final-payment attempts; grants becomes a producer into the payment system.

User notes and answers:
- Locked decisions from user: test payment is a fixed tiny global amount; confirmation destination is route-configured and may be the source thread/forum post or a specific payments channel/forum; manual admin controls for retry/hold/release/cancel/status are required; do not auto-fallback from Codex to Claude on backend connection failures.

  "watch_items": [
    "Fail-closed invariant is the top priority: any ambiguous post-submit state or confirmation timeout must land in `manual_hold`, never in auto-retry.",
    "Persist `tx_signature` and send-phase audit data before waiting on confirmation; do not leave a submitted payment recoverable only from memory.",
    "Keep `payment_requests`, `wallet_registry`, and `payment_channel_routes` off generic admin-table querying; dedicated tools must redact wallet addresses and never expose key material.",
    "Do not log, serialize, or store private keys anywhere; the provider must only hold the in-memory `SolanaClient`/keypair already used by the grants flow.",
    "Route resolution must mirror existing social-route behavior exactly: exact channel, then parent channel, then guild default; only if nothing resolves should grants fall back to the source thread.",
    "Test and final payments must remain distinct ledger entries linked by `(producer, producer_ref, is_test)` so restart and recovery logic can disambiguate phases.",
    "`release_payment` must remain the only path out of `manual_hold`; `retry_payment` must remain blocked for `submitted` and `manual_hold`.",
    "Update both the runtime logic and the admin-chat discoverability layer; adding tools without updating `agent.py` leaves the controls effectively unreachable.",
    "`payment_requested` must be treated as an active grant state everywhere that gates duplicate applications, not just in the grants cog happy path.",
    "Persistent Discord confirmations are a known verification gap in the approved plan; add lightweight automation for `PaymentConfirmView`/`bot.add_view(...)` recovery if feasible and do not regress it during implementation.",
    "Mirror the existing `social_publications` queue and `social_channel_routes` patterns rather than inventing a second queue/routing style; the repo already has debt from mixed DB access patterns.",
    "Supabase RPC behavior is a known repo-wide risk area; validate the new claim RPC carefully and keep the implementation close to the working social-publications template.",
    "Do not introduce any Codex-to-Claude automatic fallback behavior on backend failures; the user explicitly locked that out.",

  "meta_commentary": "Execute in the listed order and do not blur the safety boundaries to save time. The highest-risk mistakes are all state-machine mistakes: if a payment may have been submitted, freeze it; if the chain definitively rejected it, then and only then make it retryable.
